TEMPLATE · STATIC RENDER PROOF

Template execution lab

The shared source is parsed before this page is built. AST mode executes the committed nodes; generated mode lowers those same nodes into host-language renderers. Both modes use the same assign, define and output contract.

Cross-language proofPASS
AST modeIMPLEMENTED
Generated mode5 LANGUAGES
Page validationHTML

01 · EVIDENCE

Same bytes from AST mode

Source parsing occurs during the build. Runtime checks load the committed artifacts, apply assign and define, and compare UTF-8 output bytes. Generated mode must produce the same bytes before it can be enabled for a language.

ScenarioBytesSHA-256tsgorustphp
compiler-coverage351f9e5af2122de…PASSPASSPASSPASS
empty-state78d98cc268d4c4…PASSPASSPASSPASS
html-slot111b1fc22f8d29c…PASSPASSPASSPASS
react-boundary1244c247742a44e…PASSPASSPASSPASS
scope-precedence1777b160d439d3d…PASSPASSPASSPASS

02 · INPUT AND OUTPUT

Small SSR situations

Each card shows the exact assign data, define map, every template file, compiled artifact manifest and final HTML.

Generated compiler coverage

One readable page executes every canonical statement and expression family through AST and generated modes.

expressionsloops and loop metadatainclude scopedefine and block scope

Inputs

assign
{
  "flag": true,
  "dangerous": "&<>\"'",
  "empty_list": [],
  "empty_map": {},
  "page": {
    "title": "Guide"
  },
  "numbers": [
    10,
    20
  ],
  "lookup": {
    "x": "X"
  },
  "rows": [
    {
      "name": "A"
    },
    {
      "name": "B"
    }
  ]
}
define
{
  "layout": "layout.tpl",
  "content": {
    "template": "card.tpl",
    "data": {
      "label": "define"
    }
  }
}
templates · parser ranges
--- card.tpl
<p class="card">{= label}</p>


--- layout.tpl
{values = [0, ...numbers]}
{merged = [...lookup, 'z' => 'Z']}
<section>
<h1>{= page.title}</h1>
<p class="escaped">{= dangerous}</p>
<p class="logical">{= flag && 'x'}|{= false || 2}</p>
<p class="empty-truthiness">{= empty_list && flag}|{= empty_map && flag}</p>
<p>{= values[1]}|{= merged['z']}</p>
{? flag && page.title == 'Guide'}<strong>matched</strong>{:}<strong>missed</strong>{/}
<p>{= flag ? 'yes' : 'no'}|{= -1 + 3}|{= '' | default('fallback')}</p>
<ul>
{@ row = rows}
<li>{= row.index_}/{= row.size_}:{= row.name}:{= row.first_}:{= row.last_}</li>
{:}
<li>empty</li>
{/}
</ul>
{+ partial.tpl}
{?# content}<p>defined</p>{:}<p>missing</p>{/}
{# content label:page.title}
</section>


--- partial.tpl
<p class="included">{= values[2]}</p>

// Generated.
import { Frame, RenderContext, RuntimeBindings, RuntimeEnvironment, bind, bindMap, type MapValue, type PreparedRender, type Program, type RenderOptions, type Template, type Value } from '@polyspec/template';
export interface Page {
  title: string;
}
export interface Row {
  name: string;
}
export interface Slot {
  template?: string;
  html?: string;
}
export interface Assign {
  flag: boolean;
  dangerous: string;
  empty_list: Array<string>;
  empty_map: Map<string, string>;

fn render_layout_tpl(assign: &Assign, definitions: &Definitions, input: Input_layout_tpl) -> String { let mut out = String::new();

    let values = { let mut result: Vec<f64> = Vec::new(); result.push(0f64); result.extend(assign.numbers.clone()); result };
    let merged = { let mut result: OrderedMap<String, String> = OrderedMap::new(); for entry in assign.lookup.clone().entries() { result.set(entry.key.clone(), entry.value.clone()); } result.set("z".to_string(), "Z".to_string()); result };
    out.push_str("<section>\n<h1>");
    out.push_str(&escape(&assign.page.clone().title.to_string()));
    out.push_str("</h1>\n<p class=\"escaped\">");
    out.push_str(&escape(&assign.dangerous.clone().to_string()));
    out.push_str("</p>\n<p class=\"logical\">");
    out.push_str(&escape(&(generated_truthy(&assign.flag.clone()) && generated_truthy(&"x".to_string())).to_string()));
    out.push_str("|");
    out.push_str(&escape(&(generated_truthy(&false) || generated_truthy(&2f64)).to_string()));
    out.push_str("</p>\n<p class=\"empty-truthiness\">");
    out.push_str(&escape(&(generated_truthy(&assign.empty_list.clone()) && generated_truthy(&assign.flag.clone())).to_string()));
    out.push_str("|");
    out.push_str(&escape(&(generated_truthy(&assign.empty_map.clone()) && generated_truthy(&assign.flag.clone())).to_string()));
    out.push_str("</p>\n<p>");
    out.push_str(&escape(&values.get(1f64 as usize).cloned().unwrap_or_default().to_string()));
    out.push_str("|");
    out.push_str(&escape(&merged.get(&"z".to_string()).cloned().unwrap_or_default().to_string()));
    out.push_str("</p>\n");
    if generated_truthy(&(generated_truthy(&assign.flag.clone()) && generated_truthy(&(assign.page.clone().title == "Guide".to_string())))) {
        out.push_str("<strong>matched</strong>");    } else {
        out.push_str("<strong>missed</strong>");
    }
    out.push_str("\n<p>");
    out.push_str(&escape(&if generated_truthy(&assign.flag.clone()) { "yes".to_string() } else { "no".to_string() }.to_string()));
    out.push_str("|");
    out.push_str(&escape(&((-1f64) + 3f64).to_string()));
    out.push_str("|");
    out.push_str(&escape(&{ let value = "".to_string(); if generated_truthy(&value) { value } else { "fallback".to_string() } }.to_string()));
    out.push_str("</p>\n<ul>\n");
    { let entries = assign.rows.clone().iter().cloned().enumerate().map(|(key, value)| (key as f64, value)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    for (row_index_raw, (row_key, row_value)) in entries.iter().cloned().enumerate() {
        let row = row_value.clone();
        let row_index = row_index_raw as f64;
        let row_size = entries.len() as f64;
        let row_first = row_index_raw == 0;
        let row_last = row_index_raw + 1 == entries.len();
            out.push_str("<li>");
            out.push_str(&escape(&row_index.clone().to_string()));
            out.push_str("/");
            out.push_str(&escape(&row_size.clone().to_string()));
            out.push_str(":");
            out.push_str(&escape(&row.name.to_string()));
            out.push_str(":");
            out.push_str(&escape(&row_first.clone().to_string()));
            out.push_str(":");
            out.push_str(&escape(&row_last.clone().to_string()));
            out.push_str("</li>\n");
    }
    if entries.is_empty() {
            out.push_str("<li>empty</li>\n");
    }
    }
    out.push_str("</ul>\n");
    out.push_str(&render_partial_tpl(assign, definitions, Input_partial_tpl { values: values }));
    if definitions.content.is_some() {
            out.push_str("<p>defined</p>");
    } else {
            out.push_str("<p>missing</p>");
    }
    out.push_str("\n");
    { let definition = definitions.content.as_ref();
    if definition.is_none() { panic!("generated definition content is missing"); }
    if let Some(html) = definition.and_then(|value| value.html.as_ref()) { out.push_str(html); } else {
        let mut input = Input_card_tpl::default();

        if let Some(data) = definition.and_then(|value| value.data.as_ref()) {
            if let Some(value) = data.label.clone() { input.label = value; }
        }
        input.label = assign.page.clone().title;
        out.push_str(&render_card_tpl(assign, definitions, input));
    }
    }
    out.push_str("</section>\n");
 out }
fn render_partial_tpl(assign: &Assign, definitions: &Definitions, input: Input_partial_tpl) -> String { let mut out = String::new();

    out.push_str("<p class=\"included\">");

function generated_bind_type(mixed $value, array $type, string $path): mixed { if ($value === null) { if (($type['optional'] ?? false) || $type['kind'] === 'null' || $type['kind'] === 'any') return null; throw new InvalidArgumentException($path . ' is required'); } if ($type['kind'] === 'any') return $value; if (in_array($type['kind'], ['string', 'number', 'boolean'], true)) { $valid = $type['kind'] === 'string' ? is_string($value) : ($type['kind'] === 'number' ? ' has an unknown generated type'); }

function generated_truthy(mixed $value): bool { return $value !== null && $value !== false && $value !== '' && $value !== 0 && $value !== []; }

    $out .= "</h1>\n<p class=\"escaped\">";

type OrderedMap[K comparable, V any] struct { entries []OrderedEntry[K, V] }
func NewOrderedMap[K comparable, V any]() OrderedMap[K, V] { return OrderedMap[K, V]{} }
func (m *OrderedMap[K, V]) Set(key K, value V) { for index := range m.entries { if m.entries[index].Key == key { m.entries[index].Value = value; return } }; m.entries = append(m.entries, OrderedEntry[K, V]{key, value}) }

Scope precedence

A template definition receives root assign data, definition data and a scope argument, while a layout local stays isolated.

function generatedBindType(value, type, path) { if (value === null || value === undefined) {
    if (type.optional || type.kind === 'null' || type.kind === 'any')
        return null;
    throw new Error(path + ' is required');
} if (type.kind === 'any')
    return value; if (type.kind === 'null') {
    if (value !== null)
        throw new Error(path + ' is not null');
    return null;
} if (type.kind === 'string' || type.kind === 'number' || type.kind === 'boolean') {
    if (typeof value !== type.kind)
        throw new Error(path + ' is not a ' + type.kind);
    return value;
} if (type.kind === 'list') {
    if (!Array.isArray(value))
        throw new Error(path + ' is not a list');

    return generatedBindRecord(value, generatedRecords[type.name], path); throw new Error(path + ' has an unknown generated type'); }
function generatedBindRecord(value, fields, path, partial = false) { const object = generatedObject(value, path); const result = {}; for (const [name, type] of Object.entries(fields)) {

            throw new Error(path + '.' + name + ' is required');
        if (!partial)
            result[name] = null;
        continue;
    }
    result[name] = generatedBindType(object.get(name), type, path + '.' + name);
} return result; }

function generatedBindDefinitions(input) { const value = bind(input ?? {}); const object = generatedObject(value, 'define'); const definitions = {}; const targets = new Map(); for (const [id, raw] of object) {

            throw new Error('define.' + id + ' has an invalid template');

function render_layout_tpl(assign, definitions, input, context, runtime, rootData) {
    const frame = new Frame("layout.tpl", [0, 42, 66, 95, 106], rootData);
    const layout_local = "visible only in layout";
    context.at(frame, [42, 66]);
    context.output.write("<section class=\"scope\">\n");
    {
        const definition = definitions.content;
        if (definition === undefined)
            throw runtime.error(frame, [66, 94], 'E_RUNTIME_BLOCK_UNDEFINED', "define content is not registered");

            context.at(frame, [66, 94]);
            context.output.write(definition.html);
        }

            }
            finally {

            }
        }
    }

    context.output.write("</section>\n");
}

            return;

}

fn render_layout_tpl(assign: &Assign, definitions: &Definitions, input: Input_layout_tpl) -> String { let mut out = String::new();

    let layout_local = "visible only in layout".to_string();
    out.push_str("<section class=\"scope\">\n");
    { let definition = definitions.content.as_ref();
    if definition.is_none() { panic!("generated definition content is missing"); }
    if let Some(html) = definition.and_then(|value| value.html.as_ref()) { out.push_str(html); } else {
        let mut input = Input_content_tpl::default();
        input.root_label = assign.root_label.clone();
        input.defined_label = assign.defined_label.clone();
        if let Some(data) = definition.and_then(|value| value.data.as_ref()) {
            if let Some(value) = data.title.clone() { input.title = value; }
            if let Some(value) = data.root_label.clone() { input.root_label = value; }
            if let Some(value) = data.defined_label.clone() { input.defined_label = value; }
            if let Some(value) = data.layout_local.clone() { input.layout_local = value; }
        }
        input.title = assign.page.clone().title;
        out.push_str(&render_content_tpl(assign, definitions, input));
    }
    }
    out.push_str("</section>\n");
 out }
